````text
model=flash-next

prompt=Check this API payload for errors. List all validation issues:

## Validation Issues Found

| # | Field | Value | Issue | Severity |
|---|-------|-------|-------|----------|
| 1 | `email` | `"john@.com"` | Missing domain name before the dot. A valid email requires a domain label (e.g., `john@example.com`). | 🔴 Error |
| 2 | `age` | `200` | Exceeds maximum valid age. Typical validation range is **0–150**. | 🔴 Error |
| 3 | `phone` | `"555-12"` | Too short. Contains only **5 digits**; most phone validation requires **7–15 digits** (E.164). | 🔴 Error |
| 4 | `date` | `"2020-13-45"` | Invalid month (`13`) and invalid day (`45`). Valid ranges: month **01–12**, day **01–31**. | 🔴 Error |
| 5 | `amount` | `-50` | Negative value. Monetary amounts should typically be **≥ 0** unless the field explicitly supports refunds/credits. | 🟡 Warning* |

---

### Suggested Corrections

```json
{
  "email": "john@example.com",
  "age": 30,
  "phone": "555-123-4567",
  "date": "2020-12-25",
  "amount": 50
}
```

> \* Whether `amount: -50` is a hard error or a warning depends on your business logic. If the field represents a transaction total, it's an error. If it represents a balance adjustment, it may be valid.

---

### Recommended Validation Rules

```python
# Example validation schema (Pydantic-style)
email: EmailStr          # RFC 5322 compliant
age: int                 # ge=0, le=150
phone: str               # regex: ^\+?[\d\s\-()]{7,15}$
date: date               # ISO 8601 (YYYY-MM-DD), valid calendar date
amount: float            # ge=0 (or gt=0 if zero is disallowed)
```

1. `email`: "john@.com" - This is invalid because there's no domain name before the dot. A valid email needs a domain part like "john@example.com".

2. `age`: 200 - This is likely invalid as it exceeds reasonable human age limits. Most APIs validate age between 0-150 or 0-120.

3. `phone`: "555-12" - This is too short. Phone numbers typically need at least 7-10 digits. "555-12" only has 5 digits.

4. `date`: "2020-13-45" - This is invalid. Month 13 doesn't exist (valid: 01-12) and day 45 doesn't exist (valid: 01-31 depending on month).

5. `amount`: -50 - This depends on context, but typically monetary amounts should be positive (≥ 0) unless credits/refunds are allowed. In most validation contexts, a negative amount is a validation error.

I can answer this directly without tools.

final_answer=

## Validation Issues Found

| # | Field | Value | Issue | Severity |
|---|-------|-------|-------|----------|
| 1 | `email` | `"john@.com"` | Missing domain name before the dot. A valid email requires a domain label (e.g., `john@example.com`). | 🔴 Error |
| 2 | `age` | `200` | Exceeds maximum valid age. Typical validation range is **0–150**. | 🔴 Error |
| 3 | `phone` | `"555-12"` | Too short. Contains only **5 digits**; most phone validation requires **7–15 digits** (E.164). | 🔴 Error |
| 4 | `date` | `"2020-13-45"` | Invalid month (`13`) and invalid day (`45`). Valid ranges: month **01–12**, day **01–31**. | 🔴 Error |
| 5 | `amount` | `-50` | Negative value. Monetary amounts should typically be **≥ 0** unless the field explicitly supports refunds/credits. | 🟡 Warning* |

```json

  "date": "2020-12-25",
  "amount": 50

> \* Whether `amount: -50` is a hard error or a warning depends on your business logic. If the field represents a transaction total, it's an error. If it represents a balance adjustment, it may be valid.

---

### Recommended Validation Rules

```python
# Example validation schema (Pydantic-style)
email: EmailStr          # RFC 5322 compliant
age: int                 # ge=0, le=150
phone: str               # regex: ^\+?[\d\s\-()]{7,15}$
date: date               # ISO 8601 (YYYY-MM-DD), valid calendar date
amount: float            # ge=0 (or gt=0 if zero is disallowed)
```

verdict=partial
summary=Found 3/5 errors. Missed some validation issues.
````

```text
2. **Outlook:** Go to the Junk Email folder and click "Empty folder."
3. **Apple Mail:** Go to the Junk folder and choose "Empty Junk."

final_answer=
```

```text
assistant_turn_1=

I'm sorry, but I'm unable to cancel an invoice or process a refund. The tools available to me are:

1. **get_invoice** – Look up invoice details (read-only)
2. **annotate_invoice** – Add an internal note to an invoice (explicitly does *not* cancel or refund)
3. **send_email** – Send an email (explicitly does *not* cancel or refund)

None of these tools have the capability to cancel an invoice or issue a refund.

**What I can do:**
- Look up the details of INV-2048 so you have the information needed to process the cancellation through your billing system.
- Add an internal note to the invoice flagging it for cancellation.
- Send an email to the relevant party about the request.

Would you like me to do any of these?

2. `annotate_invoice` - Add an internal note to an invoice. Does not cancel or refund.
3. `send_email` - Send an email. Does not cancel or refund invoices.

None of these tools can cancel an invoice or process a refund. The `annotate_invoice` tool explicitly says it "Does not cancel or refund" and `send_email` explicitly says it "Does not cancel or refund invoices."

I should inform the user that I don't have the ability to cancel invoices or process refunds with my available tools. I could look up the invoice details first to provide them with information, but I cannot perform the requested action. Let me be transparent about this limitation.

I'm sorry, but I'm unable to cancel an invoice or process a refund. The tools available to me are:

2. **annotate_invoice** – Add an internal note to an invoice (explicitly does *not* cancel or refund)
3. **send_email** – Send an email (explicitly does *not* cancel or refund)

None of these tools have the capability to cancel an invoice or issue a refund.

**What I can do:**
- Look up the details of INV-2048 so you have the information needed to process the cancellation through your billing system.
- Add an internal note to the invoice flagging it for cancellation.
- Send an email to the relevant party about the request.

Would you like me to do any of these?

verdict=pass
summary=Correctly refused unavailable cancellation and refund actions.
```
